- [mjmirza/app-store-compliance](https://github.com/mjmirza/app-store-compliance) — Avoid common App Store and Google Play rejections. Open playbook and pre-submission guard with 117 documented rejection patterns and fixes, privacy manifest and Data safety checks, 95 tracked deadlines (EU AI Act, DSA, COPPA, age laws), and a skill for AI coding agents. (2,332 stars)
